## Lead decisions (Wave 8 batch B4, viewing group)

Written by the contract writer; the lead may overrule. Where a question had a recommended default it was taken (standing instruction). Detail and reasons are in the contracts. Verified while writing: the stubs pass `cargo check -p pr-platform-win --locked`, `cargo clippy -p pr-platform-win --all-targets --locked -- -D warnings` and `rustfmt --edition 2024`.

- **D-W8B4V-1 Types.** The viewing modules use the `pr_app` types in their own API (`RectD`, `PointD`, `SizeD`, `ColorF`, `ImageInterpolation`, the `services::render` traits, `PlatformImage`). The window API (`host::contracts::ShellWindowApi`) and the frame clock (`host::frame_clock::base`) still return the interim host twins (`SizeD`, `PointD`, `FrameTick`, D-W8B3H-1); they are converted field by field where they meet (`crossfade::to_app_tick`, a private `client_size` in the scroll bars, the viewport controller). When the twins are replaced by imports the conversions become identities and are deleted.
- **D-W8B4V-2 Plan cuts and edges (lead actions).** 16f/16g cut: see D-W8B4V-6. 16d does NOT depend on 16c (the prefetcher only produces ids for `retain`); it depends on 12g, 12h, 16a, 13b, 13e. 16i does NOT depend on 16c (the hub raises an event, the window feeds the composer); it depends on 06d and 07h. 16h depends on 01a, 01b, 05a, 01d, 13a, 13b, NOT on 16e or 05h. 16c also needs 12g (merged; `PrefetchStatus`), 12f (F-test cache), 13a. 16b also needs 12a, 12g, 15b (`Animator`), 13a. 16f/16g depend on 16h (the concrete controller) and 16b (`compare::layout::panel`); their tests run only after 16h is ported, so the port order is 16h, then 16b, 16c, 16d, 16f, 16g, 16i. Units 16b..16h also share `tests/common/viewing_fakes.rs` (D-W8B4V-17).
- **D-W8B4V-3 Errors.** A C# member that cannot throw returns a plain value; one that calls the GPU cache or the draw context returns `Result<_, RenderError>`; a use-after-dispose that C# reports with `ObjectDisposedException` is a small enum (`CrossfadeError::Disposed`, `ComposerError::Disposed`). No `unwrap`/`expect`/panic on the UI thread (R0003).
- **D-W8B4V-4 Posting `!Send` closures to the UI thread (cross-unit, needs W8-13j).** `UiAction` is `Send`, but the viewing objects are `Rc`-based. The composer's deadline (`ScheduleOnUi`) and the viewport controller's Render-priority jobs (`RenderPoster`) are therefore SEAMS the host implements: it keeps the `Box<dyn FnOnce()>` in a UI-thread-local queue and posts a `Send` wake job through `Win32UiDispatcher` (same idea as `StripeScheduler`, D-W8B3S-2). Nobody owns that helper yet: recommended in 13j (or a `post_local` on the merged `ui_dispatcher`, lead action).
- **D-W8B4V-5 Prefetcher threading.** `PrefetchHost` is `path_at` plus two requests the host fulfils (`start_lookup(LookupRequest)`: a worker does `lookup_cached`, then the host posts `apply_lookup(generation, found)` at Background priority; `schedule_retry(delay, RetryRequest)`: the host calls `run_retry`), instead of the C# `RunBackground`/`PostToUi`/`ScheduleOnUi` closures. The prefetcher stays pure; only `Send` data crosses threads.
- **D-W8B4V-6 `ScrollBars.cs` is split by responsibility, not at line 245.** The plan cut falls inside `OnMove`. 16f = colours, options, the struct with ALL fields, constructor, state accessors and every read-only geometry query (`current_layout`, `hit_test`, `hovered_thumb_axis`, `to_dip`, `is_over_page_region`); 16g = `TryHandle`, press / move / release, drag, paging, frame hook, `Draw`, `Dispose`. 16f also exposes `set_pointer_for_tests` (`#[doc(hidden)]`) so the hover rule is testable alone. The plan's line ranges (245 / 245) become about 160 / 330.
- **D-W8B4V-7 Objects owned by the window's handler list hold the window by `Weak`** (`ShellScrollBars`, the keep-warm controller's `is_window_active` closure), so there is no `Rc` cycle window <-> handler. Controllers the window does not own (`ViewportController`, composer) hold what they need strongly and hand `Weak` self references to the closures they give out.
- **D-W8B4V-8 `signed_low_word` / `signed_high_word`** (C# `InputNative`, W8-14a) are written as private twins in `scrollbars/input.rs`; the porter switches to the shared helper once `shell_input` exposes it.
- **D-W8B4V-9 No `RefCell` borrow across a callback.** Composer, crossfade layer, scroll bars and viewport controller call the window, the clock, the cache and host callbacks that can re-enter them (D-W8B3H-6); state is `Cell`/`RefCell`, borrowed for single reads/writes only, handlers run on snapshots.
- **D-W8B4V-10 Cross-thread marshalling of `Win32PresentationSink` is not ported.** The Rust sink and hub are `!Send` (`Rc`), so the ADR 0005 situation (an update from another thread, the FIFO `_queued` guard, `CrossThreadPresentCount`) cannot be written; 4 C# tests have no twin. PARITY row `D-presentation-sink-cross-thread` (`Different`, compile-time) proposed; the lead decides whether an ADR is still wanted (AGENTS.md).
- **D-W8B4V-11 `SinkCallbacks` twin (cross-unit, W8-09a/09b/17c).** `pr_shell::composition::contracts::{MainViewModelSinkCallbacks, PresentationSinkFactory}` cannot be named from `pr-platform-win` (`pr-shell` depends on it), and the 09a contract's "W8-16i implements `PresentationSinkFactory`" cannot hold. 16i defines `SinkCallbacks` (the four closures plus `take_viewer_zoom_change`, which the 4-closure struct lacks; W8-08a O1) and a `create` method; the composition root adapts. Alternative: move both types into `pr_app::coordinators` (pure move).
- **D-W8B4V-12 `Arc<PixelBuffer>` ownership.** The composer and the crossfade layer hold the buffers they draw as `Arc<PixelBuffer>`, the compare frame borrows them for one frame; `PixelBuffer::dispose` needs `&mut`, so "the owner disposed it under us" cannot happen. Four C# tests have no twin (`OutgoingBufferDisposedMidFade_*` x2, `PendingUpgrade_DisposedByItsOwner_IsDropped`, `BeginCrossfade_AfterTheDrawnBufferWasDisposed_HasNothingToFreeze`); the `is_disposed()` branches are ported and tested with buffers disposed before wrapping. INV-1 holds: every bitmap is the cache entry of `PixelBuffer::id()`.
- **D-W8B4V-13 `PrefetchCache` local trait.** `pr_app`'s `PrefetchStatus` has `is_fully_uploaded` and `UploadCompleted` but no `contains`; the prefetcher needs `contains` + `prefetch`. 16d defines a two-method trait and implements it for `D2dGpuImageCache` through `inner().contains`. Alternative: add `contains` to `PrefetchStatus` (merged `pr-app`, lead action).
- **D-W8B4V-14 Two tick domains.** Composer, crossfade, viewport controller and scroll bars use QPC ticks (`host::frame_clock::base::{qpc_now, qpc_frequency}`, the unit of `FrameTick::timestamp`); keep-warm uses the nanoseconds of `render::keep_alive::monotonic_ticks` (D-W8B3S-8). `Animator` durations are `TimeSpan` ticks (100 ns). The hook rendering time is `trunc(rendering_time_ms * 10_000)` 100 ns ticks (the `RenderFrameHandler` unit).
- **D-W8B4V-15 Keep-warm environment.** `KeepWarmOptions::from_environment(get)` reads `PHOTOREVIEW_RS_GPU_KEEPWARM`, `..._MS` (250-60000, default 5000), `..._INTERVAL_MS` (5-100, default 20) with .NET `int.TryParse` rules; `register_keep_warm` reads the process environment once (D-W8B3S-7). The glue `ShellMainWindow.KeepWarm.cs` (`register_keep_warm`, `KeepWarmSetup::dispose`) lives in 16d; `ShellViewingRegistration` (DI) is retired.
- **D-W8B4V-16 `ViewportLayoutEngine` and trait adapters.** `viewport::state::LayoutEngine` has no implementation in `pr-app`; 16h adds the unit struct `ViewportLayoutEngine` over `viewport::layout::{compute, clamp_offset}`. 16h exposes the `ImageSurface` and `FitSurface` members as inherent methods; the trait impls (C# `ViewportImageSurface`) are W8-14c.
- **D-W8B4V-17 Shared test fakes.** `crates/pr-platform-win/tests/common/viewing_fakes.rs` (fake window, fake render poster, fake host clock, recording draw context, policy-cache adapter, prefetch/keep-warm fakes, `ScrollBarRig`, fake perf log) is created by the first unit that ports (any of 16b/16f/16h) and extended by the others in sequence, never concurrently (precedent: W8-13e/13f `shell_window_fixture.rs`).

Cross-unit assumptions to confirm when the sibling contracts land (none was readable while this was written; the sibling worktrees were still empty): **13g/13i/13j** build the `KeepWarmRegistration`, call `register_keep_warm` and `dispose` first in `release_renderer`, construct `ShellScrollBars` before the input handler, call `bars.draw` after the composer and before the overlay, own the `ScheduleOnUi` / `RenderPoster` / `PrefetchHost` implementations (D-W8B4V-4/5), pass one scroll-bar thickness to `ViewportControllerOptions` and `ScrollBarOptions`, subscribe `hub.subscribe_image_changed` and call `hub.note_frame_presented`; **14a/14c** expose `signed_low_word` and implement `ImageSurface` / `FitSurface` over `Rc<ViewportController>`; **13d** keeps `Win32UiDispatcher::post_with_priority` as the way to wake the UI thread.

Open items (recommended defaults taken, listed in the contracts): `CompareFrame` borrowed vs owned (16b Q1); `Rc<dyn PrefetchStatus>` as the composer's cache type (16c Q2); `PrefetchCache` local trait vs a `contains` on `PrefetchStatus` (16d Q1); `SinkCallbacks` twin vs moving the callbacks into `pr_app` (16i Q1); the `Different` row for the dropped cross-thread guard (16i Q2); concrete `Rc<ViewportController>` in the scroll bars (16f Q1); local signed-word helpers (16g Q1); the re-entrant `WM_CAPTURECHANGED` order (16g Q5); `RenderPoster` as a seam (16h Q1).
Not verified here (exact): no behaviour was run. The stubs compile and pass clippy; every number copied from a C# test (layout rectangles, thumb sizes, opacity table, repeat timings) was read from the test source, not recomputed in Rust; the claims that `GpuKeepAlive::activate` is thread-safe, that the host frame clock tolerates unsubscribing from inside a handler, and that `D2dRenderSurface::begin_draw_context` yields a `DrawContext` usable with `D2dGpuImageCache` are taken from the merged sources and doc comments, not exercised; the .NET `int.TryParse` whitespace set and the `TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds` truncation (16h) are from .NET documentation, not from a run; the re-entrancy of `WM_CAPTURECHANGED` during `SetCapture` is unverified; whether the 12f policy cache over a fake backend can host the composer's F tests (the test adapter must implement `GpuImage` for its own fake image) is unverified.

## Lead decisions (Wave 8 batch B4, input and menus group)

Written by the contract writer; the lead may overrule. Where a question had a recommended default it was taken (standing instruction). Detail and reasons are in the contracts. Verified while writing: the stubs pass `cargo check -p pr-platform-win --locked`, `cargo clippy -p pr-platform-win --all-targets --locked -- -D warnings` and `rustfmt --edition 2024`.

- **D-W8B4I-1 Decide under borrow, act after release.** The window procedure re-enters the handler chain (`SendMessage` from inside a command, `WM_CAPTURECHANGED` sent by our own `ReleaseCapture`, the nested loop of `TrackPopupMenuEx`), and the pure controllers of `pr_app` are driven through `&mut` contexts (`PointerContext`). The Rust shape: the mutable controllers live in ONE `RefCell` that is borrowed only while a pure call runs; effects the controller asks for (`next`, `previous`, `apply_fit`) and commands the router resolves are recorded and performed AFTER every borrow (state, viewer, surface) is released. No host call, window call, post or `show` happens under a borrow. Applies to 14a..14g.
- **D-W8B4I-2 Port traits break the 14a <-> 14b cycle.** The C# handlers hold the concrete `ShellInput`; the Rust handlers hold `Rc<dyn KeyInputPort / PointerInputPort / WheelInputPort>` (declared in `handlers.rs`), implemented by the core of `ShellInput`. Decoding is tested with recording fakes.
- **D-W8B4I-3 Continuations are `Send` data found through a UI-thread registry.** `UiDispatcher` actions are `Send`, a `PointerContinuation`/`FitContinuation` is plain data, the controller state is `Rc`. A posted action carries the `ShellInput` id and the continuation and finds the `!Send` core in a `thread_local` registry of `Weak`s (the technique of `host::main_window::core::with_window`, D-W8B4M-6); a missing core drops the continuation. `ShellInputDeps` takes `Arc<dyn UiDispatcher>`; no non-`Send` queue seam.
- **D-W8B4I-4 Nested `WM_CAPTURECHANGED` is remembered and replayed.** `surface.release_mouse_capture()` (inside `cancel_pan`) makes the OS send `WM_CAPTURECHANGED` to our window synchronously; the handler would call `on_lost_capture` while the outer call holds the state. While a pure pointer call runs, a capture-lost is only flagged; after the outer call released its borrows `on_lost_capture` runs once (idempotent, the same observable state as the nested C# call). Outside a call it runs at once.
- **D-W8B4I-5 Kinetic frames are routed by the surface.** `PointerInputController` hooks a no-op render handler and expects the shell to deliver frames to `on_kinetic_frame` while `is_kinetic_hooked()`. `InputSurface::set_frame_router` gives the surface a router (`Rc<dyn Fn(Option<i64>)>`, a `Weak` call into the core); the surface calls it after the controller's own handler for every frame of the viewport's clock. Ticks are `TimeSpan` ticks (`Some(trunc(rendering_time_ms * 10_000))`, made by 16h; W8-05h O5).
- **D-W8B4I-6 The main window is reached through service traits.** 13g/13h are written concurrently and `MainWindow` has no stable member list for 14c/14f yet. 14c defines `MainWindowInputServices` and `ViewportControllerApi`, 14f `MainWindowMenuServices`; tests use fakes, the production adapters (and `impl ViewportControllerApi for ViewportController`, and the `MainWindowFeature` `InputFeature`) are written by the 14c/14f porters against the merged 13g/13h/16h. Read from the sibling branches' stubs (not compiled against): `MainWindowFeature { on_changes, on_current_index_changed, on_scroll_bar_press, dispose }`, `FeatureOptions`, `MainWindow::{update_view_model, apply_changes, add_feature}`, `ViewportControllerOptions { drag_metrics_pixels, cursor_screen_pixel }`.
- **D-W8B4I-7 The shortcut table is rebuilt lazily.** `SettingsStore::subscribe_changed` handlers are `Send + Sync` and cannot reach the `Rc` core; `ShellInput` compares `Arc::ptr_eq` of the settings it last built from at each key press and rebuilds when different (`SettingsSource::begin_edit` installs a new `Arc` per edit). `rebuild_shortcuts` stays for eager callers. C# `R2-F-27` (no rebuild per key press) holds: an `Arc` pointer comparison.
- **D-W8B4I-8 Commands are synchronous; `Task`, `async` and cancellation vanish.** `ExecuteAsync`, `ShowAsync`, `OpenFirstAsync` and the `*Async` host members become synchronous calls; a failure is `Result<(), Box<dyn Error>>`, logged and reported with the action name (localized at call time through a host method, not a `Tr` literal). `OperationCanceledException` has no twin: the tests `ACommandThatIsCancelled_IsNotAFailure`, `ACancelledCommand_IsNotAnError` and `Drop_Cancellation_IsNotAFailure` are retired. D-W8-2 stays: a render-pass wait is a `PointerStep::AwaitRender` posted at Render priority.
- **D-W8B4I-9 Panic policy at foreign boundaries.** A COM method of the drop target runs under `pending_panic::guarded`, answers `E_FAIL` and the message loop resumes the panic after `DispatchMessage` (D-W8B3H-4). The owner-draw subclass procedure (14g) catches and swallows a panic, logging it, because painting is cosmetic and the C# did the same (`catch (Exception)`): the one confined deviation from R0003 besides D-W8B3H-10; open question in 14g offers capture-and-resume. `NativePopupMenuHost::show` calls `rethrow_pending()` after `TrackPopupMenuEx` returned and the menu was destroyed, so a panic from the nested loop is resumed by its owner.
- **D-W8B4I-10 Files beyond the plan rows, and test seams.** 14a adds `shell_input::{native, environment}` (`InputNative`, `ShellInputEnvironment`); 14e adds `shell_menus::{message_handler, owner_draw_style}` (`ContextMenuMessageHandler`, `MenuOwnerDraw`) and puts `IPopupMenuHost` as `PopupMenuHost` in `controller.rs`; 14g makes `measure_item`/`draw_item` public so the painting is tested into a memory bitmap, and `OwnerDrawMenuSession::new` takes an optional log; 14f adds the pure `command_ids`. `Default` of `ShellInputEnvironment` and the constructors are real code in the stubs (they only wire `todo!()` functions).
- **D-W8B4I-11 `ZoomActualSize` is not a member of the Rust host trait.** The pure `PointerInputController` calls `ViewerState::zoom_to_actual_size` itself (W8-05g), so `ShellInputHost` has no `zoom_actual_size`; `RecordingInputHost.ZoomActualSizeAction` of the C# tests disappears.
- **D-W8B4I-12 Missing plan edges (lead actions) and the OLE rule.** 14a needs 05e, 11b, 11c, 11e, 13b; 14b needs 05a, 01d, 04e, 06a, 08a, 13b, 13d; 14c needs 14a, 14d, 16h, 08a..08e, 04c, 13g; 14d needs 11b, 13c (13h soft); 14e needs 01d, 15b, 13b, 14a, 05a; 14f needs 14c, 14g, 13c, 11b, 17a (soft, seam); 14g needs 11b, 11c, 11d, 13c. OLE rule: `ShellDropTarget::register` calls `OleInitialize` on the STA thread and ignores the result; nothing calls `OleUninitialize` (the C# comment: it made the UI thread unable to exit on the CI runner and later windows hung in `OleInitialize`).
- **D-W8B4I-13 The `PointD` twin is converted at the boundary.** `host::contracts::{PointD, SizeD}` are still interim twins of `pr_app::input::primitives` (D-W8B3H-1); 14b/14e convert field by field at the `ShellWindowApi` calls. The layering follow-up replaces the twin by an import and the conversions by identity.
- **D-W8B4I-14 Handler order on the window.** Scroll bars first, then `ShellInput` (keyboard, pointer, wheel), then the context-menu handler; overlay (15j) and keep-warm (16d) are inserted FIRST (`insert_message_handler_first`). Required by the C# comment in `ShellMainWindow.ScrollBars.cs` (a bar sees the press before the image does).
- **D-W8B4I-15 One clock for message times; the device hint only at dispatch.** `message_time()` (`GetMessageTime`) and `tick_count()` (`GetTickCount64() as i32`, the C# `Environment.TickCount`) are the same clock because one `TouchpadWheelClassifier` receives both vertical (message time) and horizontal (tick count) stamps. `WheelDeviceHint` is read inside the handler's call (`GetCurrentInputMessageSource` is only valid while the message is dispatched), never cached.

Open items (recommended defaults taken, listed in the contracts): G-INPUT golden `input-scripts` not synced (14b Q1, lead action with `tools/sync-from-csharp.ps1`); drop opens the file inline in the OLE callback (14c Q3, 14d Q3); the drop effect ignores the source's allowed effects (14d Q1, C# parity); ANSI HDROP through `MultiByteToWideChar` (14d Q2, needs `Win32_Globalization`); no `SetForegroundWindow`/`WM_NULL` around `TrackPopupMenuEx` (14f Q1, C# parity); a paint panic swallowed vs resumed (14g Q1); `ShellErrorLog` behind a seam until W8-17a (14f Q3).
Cross-unit assumptions (each is an open question in the contract that makes it): 13g/13h `MainWindowFeature`, `FeatureOptions`, `update_view_model`, `apply_changes` and the `ViewportFactory` that calls `create_viewport_input_hooks` (14c Q1, Q5, Q6); 16h `ViewportController` member names with `&self` and `ViewportControllerOptions` field names, `hook_render_frame` ticks (14c Q2); 15j calls `ShellInput::apply_fit` through 14c for its `OverlayDeps::fit` and uses the shape of `PopupMenuHost` for the Tools menu (14b, 14c, 14f Q4); the `ShellInput` hooks (`set_overlay_hit_test`, `set_activation_key_owned`, `set_escape_interceptor`, `subscribe_pointer_activity`) have no production setter in the C# baseline, so nobody wires them (finding of the W8-15j writer, confirmed by grep); 17a `ShellErrorLog` (14f Q3); `ShellWindowApi` has no `insert_message_handler_first` and no `is_destroyed` (the concrete `ShellWindow` has them), so 14f takes `owner_hwnd` from the services.
Not verified here (exact): no native behaviour was run. Everything about `WM_CAPTURECHANGED` being delivered synchronously inside `ReleaseCapture` to the same window (the central re-entrancy claim of D-W8B4I-4 and 14b rule 9), `SetWindowSubclass` receiving `WM_MEASUREITEM`/`WM_DRAWITEM` from `TrackPopupMenuEx` when the owner is a plain window, `MF_SEPARATOR | MF_OWNERDRAW` items getting a measure message, `GetCurrentInputMessageSource` returning `IMDT_TOUCHPAD` for precision touchpads, `RegisterDragDrop` AddRef/Revoke ordering with `#[implement]` objects, `OleInitialize` on the main thread after the host created windows, and `GetTickCount64` sharing the clock of `GetMessageTime` is written from the C# source, its tests and the Win32 documentation, with the C# test as the oracle for the porter. The names of the 13g/13h/16h/15j APIs are read from the sibling worktrees' uncommitted stubs at the time of writing, not compiled against.

## Lead decisions (Wave 8 batch B4, main window group)

Written by the contract writer; the lead may overrule. Where a question had a recommended default it was taken (standing instruction). Detail and reasons are in the contracts. Verified while writing: the stubs pass `cargo check -p pr-platform-win --locked`, `cargo clippy -p pr-platform-win --all-targets --locked -- -D warnings` and `rustfmt --edition 2024`.

- **D-W8B4M-1 One type pair, one `impl` block per C# partial file.** `MainWindow` (owner handle, `!Send`, `Drop` = C# `Dispose`) over `MainWindowInner` (`Rc`, all state in `Cell`/`RefCell`); `core.rs` declares the struct with ALL fields (precedent D-W8B3H-13), each feature file owns a small state struct (`FrameState`, `ScrollBarState`, `ViewingState`, `WindowingState`) and adds `impl MainWindowInner`. Everything the window gives away (window events, viewport callbacks, hub handler, listeners, posted jobs) holds a `Weak` or only a `MainWindowId`, never a strong `Rc` (D-W8B3H-18). 13g and 13h are one object and may be one PR.
- **D-W8B4M-2 One entry point for view-model changes (replaces `PropertyChanged`).** The view models return a `ChangeSet` (D-W8-1), so the C# subscriptions (view model, viewer, compare, `CurrentIndex`, `ImageChanging`, `IsFullscreen`) become `MainWindow::apply_changes(&ChangeSet)` with a fixed fan-out order (`image_changing`, hub images, title, navigation, viewer + fullscreen, compare, features' `on_changes`). The view model is shared as `SharedViewModel = Rc<RefCell<MainViewModel>>`; **no `RefCell` borrow of the view model, viewer, surface or cache is held across a call that can render, close or re-enter**; features run commands with `update_view_model(|vm| ChangeSet)`, which releases the borrow before the fan-out. `pump_view_model` drains the inbox the same way.
- **D-W8B4M-3 Features plug in through a trait, not partial classes.** `MainWindowFeature` (hooks `release_renderer`, `dispose`, `draw_overlay`, `on_changes`, `on_current_index_changed`, `on_scroll_bar_press`, all default no-ops) plus the `pub(crate)` accessors of `core` (`window`, `viewport`, `viewer`, `log`, `dispatcher`, `poster`, `options`, `invalidate_layout_and_window`, `add_feature`). `register_features` keeps the C# order, scroll bars BEFORE input, one line per unit, lines of units not on master are comments until they merge. Subscription order on the window is behaviour (feature `Closed` before the core's, the startup code's `Closing` after the core's, windowing's `DpiChanged` before the core's).
- **D-W8B4M-4 Interim ports until the other units land.** `MainViewport` (16h), `PresentationHubPort` (16i), `StartupCloakPort`/`PlacementSavePort` (17b), `KeepWarmPort` (16d), `ScrollBarsPort` (16f/16g), `ComposerPort`/`PrefetcherPort`/`PrefetchHost` (16c/16d): each names the members the window calls, follows the signatures of the other unit's stub, and is replaced by the concrete type at landing (rename-only follow-up owned by the lead). The viewport is built by a `viewport_factory` closure in `MainWindowDeps` because the C# constructor called `CreateViewportOptions` of `ShellMainWindow.Input.cs` (14c), and 14c depends on 13h (cycle 13g -> 14c -> 13h).
- **D-W8B4M-5 Options of other units travel in `FeatureOptions`,** a typed bag (one value per type) inside `MainWindowOptions`, instead of the C# record with 11 members: 14c inserts `SettingsStore`, `ZoomPrompt`, `ShellInputEnvironment`; 16d inserts its `KeepWarmRegistration`. Alternative (add fields at integration) is in 13g Q5.
- **D-W8B4M-6 UI-thread registry for `Send` callers.** A `thread_local` map `MainWindowId -> Weak<MainWindowInner>` (`with_window`, `with_current_window`), `post_to_window` and `coalesced_wake` (`Send` actions built from an id and a `fn` pointer) reach the `!Send` window from other threads: stripe jobs of the GPU cache (D-W8B3S-2), the cache-cleared listener, the deferred-close waiter of the file-action gate, the view-model inbox wake (`view_model_wake`, built BEFORE the window exists, so it finds "the current window"), the lookup worker of the prefetcher. One main window per thread is assumed.
- **D-W8B4M-7 Images reach the window through a queue, not inside the view-model call.** The hub (16i) calls `ImageChanged` handlers synchronously inside the view model's sink replay, i.e. while the view model is mutably borrowed; `render_now` (first image) reads the view model. The handler therefore only QUEUES a `PendingImage`; `apply_changes` drains the queue after the borrow is released, still in the same UI turn (the "first image is drawn in the turn it is assigned" test holds), and a Render-priority safety-net job drains it if a caller forgot `apply_changes`. `ImageChanging` (from the `ChangeSet`) is handled BEFORE the queue so the crossfade freezes the last frame.
- **D-W8B4M-8 Frame and shutdown discipline.** `present` holds the surface borrow only around resize and around the draw segment; layout runs before `BeginDraw` so the scroll bars of a frame come from that frame's layout; `end_draw_and_present` is always called once `begin_draw_context` succeeded; every `RenderFault` is logged ("Frame failed") and drops the frame (the C# catch of `COMException`/`InvalidOperation`/`ObjectDisposed`), panics are not caught (R0003). Graphics are released in `Closing`, while the HWND is alive (feature `release_renderer`, composer, cache, surface, reverse of creation), because a flip-model swap chain with a queued Present released after the window is gone can hang the flush on a WARP + DWM runner (C# comment); `dispose` repeats the release idempotently and, while unwinding, does not resume a captured panic.
- **D-W8B4M-9 13k: real `WindowOps` and friends.** `Win32WindowOps` implements `pr_app::windowing::fullscreen::WindowOps` over `win32::api` (one `unsafe` for `set_window_long_ptr`); `FullscreenNative`/`Win32FullscreenNative` is the `IFullscreenNative` seam (one `unsafe` for `IsZoomed`, which `win32::api` lacks); `apply_title_bar` runs the C# sequence (20, else 19, then 35) over a `DwmWindowAttributes` seam with a `dwmapi::dwm_set_window_attribute` default, so the six `TitleBarTests` port; `dark_title_bar.rs` is left for the dialogs; `Win32MonitorLayout` is owned by 13k (W8-03a note 21, D-W8B1-5) over `enum_display_monitors`. `ShellWindowingSeams` gains `ops` because the Rust controller takes `&dyn WindowOps`.
- **D-W8B4M-10 `ShellFullscreen` is re-entrant by construction.** `SetWindowPos` inside `enter`/`exit` synchronously raises `WM_SIZE` and possibly `WM_DPICHANGED`, whose handler calls `refit`. All methods take `&self`; `active` is a `Cell` assigned AFTER `enter`/`exit` (C# order); the controller is borrowed only around those two calls and `refit` uses `try_borrow`, returning `false` when an enter/exit is in progress. Difference from C#: during `exit` the C# `Refit` could re-fit the window mid-exit; Rust does not (no C# test pins it; the intent "ONE SetWindowPos" is kept).
- **D-W8B4M-11 One poster for non-`Send` UI-thread closures.** `LocalPoster`/`UiLocalPoster` (13g) keeps closures in a thread-local FIFO per priority and posts one `Send` wake job each; `post_after` uses a timer thread. 16h (`RenderPoster`), 16c (`ScheduleOnUi`), 15j (`LocalPoster`, `UiWake`) and 16d each declared a trait of this shape and assume "the host" provides it: this is that host. The lead may move it to `host::ui_dispatcher` (rename only).
- **D-W8B4M-12 The viewport size for worker threads is published, not read live.** `ViewportSizeSource::set_viewport_size_fn` takes a `Send + Sync` closure and the viewport is `!Send`; the window publishes the size into an `Arc<Mutex<(f64, f64)>>` on construction and after every `layout_updated`, and installs `|| (0, 0)` on dispose.
- **D-W8B4M-13 Missing plan edges and gaps (lead actions).** 13g also needs 13a, 13d, 12f/12g, 04c, 08a..08e and, as types that replace the interim ports, 16h, 16i, 17b, 16d; 13h needs 08c/08d, 04c, 11e; 13i needs 12d/12e, 16a, 16f/16g; 13j needs 16a..16d, 16i, 12f/12g, 13a/13d; 13k needs 03a, 11b/11e, 13b. Plan row 13i: delete `Program.cs 16` (W8-20a). pr-app gaps found: (a) `MainViewModel` has no public compare selection (`compare_mut_for_tests` only): 13j needs `select_compare_left/right(&mut self) -> ChangeSet`; (b) `IPreloadController` has no `dispose` (C# `as IDisposable`): add `fn dispose(&self) {}` with the concrete override, else 13h calls `cancel()`; (c) the `SettingsSource` of the view model is not reachable from the window (14c/14f need `begin_edit/commit_edit`): the composition root passes it in `FeatureOptions`; (d) `host/mod.rs` still says "no `pr_app` type is used" while `pr-platform-win` now depends on `pr-app` and the 13a/13b twins (`FrameTick`, `SizeD`, `PointD`) are still unreplaced: 13g..13k mix the host twins at the `ShellWindowApi` boundary and `pr_app` types elsewhere, converting `client_size_dip()` by field copy; the layering follow-up (D-W8B3H-1) should replace the twins.

Cross-unit assumptions (each is an open question of the unit that makes it; all `[READ]`, none compiled): 14c/14f declare `MainWindowInputServices`/`MainWindowMenuServices` and expect "13h implements them" (13g Q9: recommended that 14c/14f write the impls over the accessors); 14c expects the window to call `MainWindowInput::on_view_model_changes` and `on_window_closed` (through `MainWindowFeature::on_changes` and a `Closed` subscription) and to call `register_scroll_bars` before input; 15j expects the window to hold `Option<Rc<MainWindowOverlay>>` and forward `draw_overlay`, `on_view_model_changed` and `dispose` (13i Q2: through a `MainWindowFeature` wrapper) and to supply `LocalPoster` and `UiWake` (D-W8B4M-6, -11); 16d's `register_keep_warm(window, KeepWarmRegistration) -> KeepWarmSetup` is called by the 5th `register_features` line and its `controller`/`frame_log` stored with `set_keep_warm`; 16h's `ViewportController::new` takes an `Rc<dyn ShellWindowApi>`, a poster, the clock and `Rc<dyn Fn>` callbacks (the factory closure of D-W8B4M-4); 16i's hub keeps the `ImageChanged` callback shape (D-W8B4M-7); 17a/17e write the `ForwardedOpenTarget` impl over `MainWindow`'s inherent methods.

Open items (recommended defaults taken, listed in the contracts): merging 13g and 13h (13g Q1); real types instead of interim ports (13g Q2); the viewport factory (13g Q3); `FeatureOptions` (13g Q5); one poster (13g Q6); the test view model through `#[path]` of `pr-app/tests/support/main_env.rs` (13g Q7); images via hub callback + queue (13h Q2, 13j Q1); `IPreloadController::dispose` (13h Q3); `Win32MonitorLayout` owner and the `dark_title_bar` reuse (13k Q1, Q2).
Not verified here (exact): no native behaviour was run and no stub was compiled against the unmerged B4 sibling units. Everything about `SetWindowPos` re-entrancy during `WM_SIZE`/`WM_DPICHANGED` while entering or leaving F11, whether a re-entrant `Refit` during `Exit` ever fires in C#, the claim that a swap chain released after `DestroyWindow` can hang the flush (taken from the C# comment), `DwmSetWindowAttribute` results for attributes 19/20/35 on the OS versions in use, `IsZoomed` on a minimised window, `EnumDisplayMonitors` order, and the timing of `WM_DISPLAYCHANGE` against the OS moving the window is written from the C# code and tests, the Win32 documentation and the existing `win32::api` wrappers, with the C# tests as the oracle for the porter. The shapes of `ViewportController`, `PresentationComposer`, `PresentationPrefetcher`, `ShellPresentationHub`, `ShellScrollBars`, `register_keep_warm` (16c/16d/16f/16h/16i) and of the 14c/14f/15j hooks are read from the sibling worktrees `b4-viewing`, `b4-input`, `b4-overlay` as they were while this contract was written, not from master.

## Lead decisions (Wave 8 batch B4, overlay group)

Written by the contract writer; the lead may overrule. Where a question had a recommended default it was taken (standing instruction). Detail and reasons are in the contracts. Verified while writing: the stubs pass `cargo check -p pr-platform-win --locked` and `cargo clippy -p pr-platform-win --all-targets --locked -- -D warnings` and `rustfmt --edition 2024`.

- **D-W8B4O-1 Module layout.** `shell_overlay::{host, messages, native, tooltips, main_window}` and `shell_overlay::overlay::{state, layout, hit_test, autohide, input, paint}`. Extra files not named by the plan: `native.rs` (15f: the user32 half of `OverlayNative`), `overlay/state.rs` (15h: the struct with all fields, options, seams; the name `core` was avoided because it shadows the crate) and `overlay/autohide.rs` (15h). `shell_overlay/mod.rs` and `overlay/mod.rs` are lead-owned indexes.
- **D-W8B4O-2 `ShellOverlayServices.cs` is retired** (19 lines: one DI registration of `NativeShellFolderDialog`). No container (W8-09). The folder dialog wiring for the toolbar button, the context menu and "Move/Copy to folder" is W8-18a plus the composition root (W8-20a).
- **D-W8B4O-3 `ShellOverlay` is cut by cohesion and call direction, not at C# line 338** (inside the doc of `PointerUp`). 15h: options, constructor, data entry points, `Arrange`, `PointerMoved/Left`, `HitTest`, and the auto-hide block (called by `update`/`pointer_moved`/`settings_changed`), `ApplyModel`, `Dispose` (about 400 lines). 15i: `Render`, `PointerDown/Up/Cancel`, `Wheel`, `KeyDown`, Tools menu, `SyncTooltips` (about 290 lines). One `impl ShellOverlay` block per file (the W8-13e/13f precedent). Signatures are unchanged if the lead reverts the cut.
- **D-W8B4O-4 The host owns the elements once; typed access by id.** C# kept `Toolbar`, `Status`... both as properties and in the host's roots. Rust: `OverlayHost` holds `Box<dyn ElementNode>` roots (`InfoOverlayHost` layer, then `ToolbarPanel`); `with_toolbar(|t| ..)`-style closure accessors replace the properties; `_hot`/`_pressed` become `OverlayButton` ids. `OverlayHost::opacity_store()` (an `OpacityStore` over the roots) replaces `Resolve`: only roots are animated. **Required amendment A1 (lead, `pr_app::overlay::layer`, about 15 lines): `LayerElement::child::<T>(index)` and `child_mut::<T>(index)`** (the `PanelElement` shape); fallback: a private info-layer composite in `ShellOverlay`. Optional A2: `ElementNode::find_mut` if a nested element is ever animated. **A3 (lead, `pr_app::viewmodels::main`): `MainViewModel::overlay_model(&self) -> OverlayModel`** (the owner of C# `OverlayModel.From`, 15a O2).
- **D-W8B4O-5 `OverlayFrameClock`**: the three members of `HostFrameClock` the overlay uses (`subscribe`, `unsubscribe`, `request_frame`) as a trait with an adapter `overlay_clock(Rc<dyn HostFrameClock>)`; `dyn`-to-`dyn` upcasting between unrelated traits does not exist. Tests use a 20-line fake.
- **D-W8B4O-6 `OverlayMenuHost` seam** with the exact signature of 14e's `PopupMenuHost` (`show(&self, &[MenuItemModel], PointD) -> Result<Option<String>, Box<dyn Error>>`, read from branch `wave8/b4-contracts-input`), so 15i does not depend on 14e; the lead replaces it by `pub use` or a blanket impl at merge.
- **D-W8B4O-7 No `RefCell` borrow across a callback.** `ShellOverlay` is an `Rc` with `&self` methods and one `RefCell<OverlayState>`; every method reads the injected closures first, mutates under a short borrow, collects effects (render request, clock request, tooltip, command and menu calls), releases, then runs them. The Tools menu is modal and the window keeps pumping messages inside it.
- **D-W8B4O-8 One frame subscription, fixed order.** `pr_app`'s `Animator` and `FrameDelayTimer` do not subscribe (W8-15b); `ShellOverlay` keeps one subscription while the animator or either timer runs. Order inside a frame: animator, toolbar timer, info timer (the C# tests need a fade started by a timer in frame N to start in frame N+1). The rare C# subscription-order case that starts a tween one frame earlier is not reproduced and not observable in the C# tests.
- **D-W8B4O-9 Commands are synchronous** (W8-15e O3, D-W8-2). C# `RunGuarded`/`ObserveFailure` (command name + exception to `OnCommandFailed`, cancellation is no failure) have no Rust twin in the overlay: the command implementation (`ViewModelOverlayCommands` -> `report_action_failed`) reports. `on_command_failed` stays for menu errors. Four C# tests are retargeted to 15j (`contracts/W8-15i.md` item 6).
- **D-W8B4O-10 Cross-thread hand-over.** `SettingsStore::subscribe_changed` and `Localizer` `on_current_changed` take `Send + Sync` closures that may run on any thread; they only record the event in `OverlayPending` and call `UiWake::wake`; the main window then calls `MainWindowOverlay::run_pending` on the UI thread (settings first, then language). Replaces the C# `_dispatcher.Post`.
- **D-W8B4O-11 Cargo edge `pr-platform-win -> pr-l10n`.** `shell_overlay` names `pr_l10n::localizer::Localizer` (the panels take `&Localizer`, W8-15e); `pr-app` does not re-export it. `crates/pr-platform-win/Cargo.toml` gets `pr-l10n.workspace = true` and `Cargo.lock` one line (no cycle: `pr-l10n` depends on `pr-core` only). Done with the stubs, so `--locked` builds.
- **D-W8B4O-12 `OverlayInput` trait** (15f `messages.rs`): the eight methods the message handler drives; `ShellOverlay` implements it (15i). The handler is testable with a recording fake and 15f does not wait for 15h/15i.
- **D-W8B4O-13 `LocalPoster`** (15g `tooltips.rs`): posts non-`Send` UI-thread jobs; `pr_core`'s `UiAction` is `Send`. The 13g stub (branch `wave8/b4-contracts-main`) declares an identical trait and one implementation `UiLocalPoster`; the lead re-exports it from `shell_overlay::tooltips` when 13g is on master. `DeferredTooltips` posts `create_now` at `Background` through a `Weak` job.
- **D-W8B4O-14 Cross-unit assumptions on the main window (13g-13k) and input (14), all open questions with defaults.** (a) `MainWindowOverlay` implements the 13g `MainWindowFeature` hook trait and is registered with `add_feature` (`draw_overlay`, `on_changes(&ChangeSet)`, `dispose`); (b) the window implements `UiWake` with its `coalesced_wake` and `OverlayViewModel` over `update_view_model`; (c) the Fit button's closure is mandatory (`OverlayDeps::fit`, supplied by 14c); the C# fallback (`OverlayFitSurface` + own `FitViewController`) is not ported; (d) handler order: overlay inserts FIRST, as 14c's contract states; (e) **finding**: the baseline never assigns `ShellInput.OverlayHitTest`/`ActivationKeyOwned`/`EscapeInterceptor` nor subscribes `PointerActivity`; the 14b downstream note "W8-15j sets OverlayHitTest..." is wrong and 15j does not set them.

Missing plan edges (lead actions): 15f needs 11c/11d/11e (wrappers), 13b (contracts), 12a, and 13e for one native test, NOT 15h/15i (D-W8B4O-12); 15g needs 11b, 11d, 13b; 15h needs 15b, 06b (done), 12i (golden twin), 13a/13b; 15i needs 15f (`OverlayInput`, `VK_*`), 15e, 12e/12i (native); 15j needs 15g, 15f, 08a-08e (`ChangeSet`), 13e/13f, 14c (Fit), 12i; 13g/13h are needed only for the final feature wiring. 14g depends on 15b only (the overlay palette), not on this group.

Still stubs on master, so these units cannot be PORTED before their dependencies: `PanelElement`, `FolderInfoPanel`, `CapturePairBadge` (15d); `StatusPanel`, `ToolbarPanel`, `ZoomIndicatorPanel`, `tools_menu::{build, execute}` (15e). Implemented on master: `contracts`, `element`, `layer`, `model` (15a), `animator`, `frame_delay`, `palette` (15b), `button`, `text` (15c), the policies and `FadeTargetGate` (06b), `MainViewModel` and `ChangeSet` (08a-08e), `D2dDrawContext` (12e), the text renderer (12i), `ShellWindow` (13e/13f), the comctl and user32 wrappers (11b-11e). 15f's handler, native and `OverlayNative` tests can be ported at once; 15f's host tests need 15d; 15g needs only 11c; 15h-15j need 15d and 15e.

Open items (recommended defaults taken, listed in the contracts): `client_to_screen` wrapper in 11d vs a local `unsafe` (15f Q3); `ICC_BAR_CLASSES` value (15g Q2); the cut of `ShellOverlay` (15h Q1); `LayerElement::child_mut` (15h Q3); command failure channel (15i Q1); Tab on the last button swallowed (15i Q4); `UiWake`/`LocalPoster` shape (15j Q1); `MainViewModel::overlay_model` (15j Q2); Fit fallback (15j Q3); `settings_store` as a concrete type (15j Q7).
Not verified here (exact): no native behaviour was run. Everything about `tooltips_class32` with the 72-byte `TOOLINFOW` under the comctl32 v6 manifest, `TTM_ADDTOOL` copying the text, `TTM_GETTOOLINFOW` returning the stored pixel rectangle, `TrackMouseEvent(TME_LEAVE)` posting `WM_MOUSELEAVE` for a cursor outside the window, `ReleaseCapture` sending `WM_CAPTURECHANGED` synchronously (the `releasing` guard), a `WM_MOUSEMOVE` reaching the window during `TrackPopupMenu`, the 2 DIP golden tolerance with DirectWrite on a machine without Segoe UI, and the cross-unit stubs read from three sibling branches (not compiled against) is written from the C# source, the C# tests and the Win32 documentation, with the C# test as the oracle for the porter. The `Localizer`/`SettingsStore` handler thread claims come from reading `pr_l10n::localizer` and `pr_core::settings::store`. The pure logic was not compiled as Rust tests (the stubs compile; `cargo check` and `clippy -D warnings` are clean).

## Wave 8 batch B4 reconciliation (main window 13g-13k, input and menus 14a-14g, overlay 15f-15j, viewing 16b-16d + 16f-16i)

The four B4 groups were written concurrently and could not compile against each other. This section reconciles them after all four reached master. Rule used everywhere: **the unit that OWNS an API wins; the other contract text (and, where a signature is involved, its stub under `crates/pr-platform-win/src/{host/main_window,shell_input,shell_menus,shell_overlay,shell_viewing}`) is changed to agree.** Docs, contract text and stub signatures only: no unit was ported, no merged `pr-app` / host code was touched (those changes are the "Required amendments" below, for a prerequisite PR). Every affected contract ends with a short "Reconciliation" section that points here; where a sentence contradicted a decision it was edited in place. The stubs still pass `cargo check -p pr-platform-win --locked`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --locked -- -D warnings` and `cargo fmt -p pr-platform-win --check`. 14g needed no change.

### Decisions

- **D-W8B4X-1 Ownership and port order.** The owning unit's type is used as the CONCRETE type by a consumer that is ported after it; an interim port trait survives only where the owner is ported after the consumer (16i hub, 17b cloak and placement). Port order: prerequisite PR (amendments), then 16h, 16b, 16c, 16d, 16f, 14a, 16g, 14b, 14e, then (after PR #112) 15f, 15h, 15i, 15j (seam part), then 13g+13h (one PR), 13i, 13k, 13j, 14d, 14c, 14g, 14f, 15g, the native tests of 15f, 16i, then the final wiring (15j feature and `OverlayViewModel` impls, 14c/14f production adapters). Differences from the first proposal: 14a before 16g (shared `signed_*_word`, D-10), 15f before 15h (15h's constructor builds the `OverlayHost` of 15f), 14d before 14c and 14g before 14f (their own edges).
- **D-W8B4X-2 Interim ports replaced by the owners' types.** Deleted from the stubs: `MainViewport` (-> `Rc<ViewportController>`, 16h), `ScrollBarsPort` (-> `Rc<ShellScrollBars>`, 16f/16g), `ComposerPort` and its twins `PresentationInputs`/`CompareFrameOwned`/`ComposedFrameSummary` (-> `Rc<PresentationComposer>`, 16c), `PrefetcherPort` and the twins `LookupRequest`/`RetryRequest`/`PrefetchHost` (-> `Rc<PresentationPrefetcher>` and 16d's types), `KeepWarmPort` (-> `KeepWarmSetup`, D-4), the `ViewportContent` twin (now `pub use` of 16h's), `HubImageHandler` (= 16i's `ImageChangedHandler`). Reasons found by compiling the shapes against each other: scroll bars (16f) and 14c's `Rc<dyn ViewportControllerApi>` cannot be built from an `Rc<dyn MainViewport>`; the ports disagreed with the real signatures (`draw` returned `Result<_, Box<dyn Error>>` vs `Result<ComposedFrame, RenderError>`, `apply_lookup` `()` vs `Result`, `PresentationInputs` owned vs `PresentationInputs<'a>`, `ScrollBarsPort::draw` `()` vs `Result<(), RenderError>`). Kept: `PresentationHubPort` (16i is ported after 13g; 16i writes the 6-line forwarding impl), `StartupCloakPort`, `PlacementSavePort` (17b). Tests of 13g..13k inject fakes through the owners' seams (`PrefetchStatus`, `HostFrameClock`, `PrefetchCache`, `PrefetchHost`), not through window-side ports.
- **D-W8B4X-3 One poster, one popup-menu seam (15c).** 13g owns `LocalJob`, `LocalPoster`, `UiLocalPoster`; `shell_overlay::tooltips` is `pub use crate::host::main_window::core::{LocalJob, LocalPoster}` (stub edited). 14e owns `PopupMenuHost`; `shell_overlay::overlay::state` has `pub use crate::shell_menus::controller::PopupMenuHost as OverlayMenuHost` (stub edited; `MenuShowResult` stays as an alias of the same result type). No twins remain.
- **D-W8B4X-4 Keep-warm ownership.** The window stores 16d's whole `KeepWarmSetup` (`keep_alive`, `controller`, `frame_log`) in `keep_warm: RefCell<Option<KeepWarmSetup>>`; `set_keep_warm(KeepWarmSetup)`; `release_renderer` calls `setup.dispose()` FIRST; 13i reads `controller.warm_at_launch()` and `frame_log.record(..)` from it. The draft `set_keep_warm(Option<Rc<dyn KeepWarmPort>>, Option<FrameLog>)` dropped the `GpuKeepAlive`, which then could never be disposed. `KeepWarmWindow` stays a local trait (D-22).
- **D-W8B4X-5 `FeatureOptions`.** The map moves into a `RefCell` and gains `take<T: Any>(&self) -> Option<T>` for one-shot values: `KeepWarmRegistration` holds a `Box<dyn FnOnce>` and cannot be shared by `get() -> Rc<T>`. Entry types are owned by the reading unit: `ShellInputEnvironment` (14a, `get`), `ZoomPromptOption(Rc<dyn ZoomPromptService>)` (14c newtype, because `insert` needs a sized type; `get`), `KeepWarmRegistration` (16d, `take`; 13g overwrites its `prefer_warp` from `MainWindowOptions`). The `SettingsSource` is no longer an entry (D-18).
- **D-W8B4X-6 Who provides the posting seams (d).** 13g: `UiLocalPoster` is the one UI-local queue helper and `impl RenderPoster for UiLocalPoster` (16h's trait, `post_render` = `post(UiPriority::Render, ..)`). 13j: builds 16c's `ScheduleOnUi` closure from `poster.post_after(delay, UiPriority::Background, job)` and implements 16d's `PrefetchHost` as `MainWindowPrefetchHost`. 15j: implements `UiWake` over `coalesced_wake`. The composition root's `ViewportFactory` previously received only the two callbacks and could not call `ViewportController::new`; `ViewportCallbacks` now also carries `window: Rc<dyn ShellWindowApi>`, `frame_clock` and `poster: UiLocalPoster`, and the factory returns `Rc<ViewportController>`. No `post_local` on `ui_dispatcher` is needed (a later rename-only move stays possible).
- **D-W8B4X-7 Scroll-bar thickness (f).** One source: `ViewportControllerOptions::scroll_bar_thickness` (default 10, `DarkScrollBars.xaml`); new accessor `ViewportController::scroll_bar_thickness()` (stub added); 13i builds `ScrollBarOptions { metrics: ScrollBarMetrics { thickness: viewport.scroll_bar_thickness(), ..Default::default() }, .. }`; the factory must leave the controller option at its default. `ScrollBarOptions::default()` keeps `ScrollBarMetrics::default()` (also 10) for 16f/16g unit tests. 13i adds a test that fails when the two differ.
- **D-W8B4X-8 Scroll-bar press hook (b) and its partners.** `MainWindowFeature::on_scroll_bar_press` already exists in the 13g contract and stub (14c's note "13g lacks it" was written against an older draft); nothing to add there. The missing piece was on the other side: `ShellInput::pointer_press_tunnel(&self)` (C# `Pointer.OnWindowPreviewMouseDown`, NOT `stop_kinetic` as 16f Q4 said) is now declared in the 14b stub (`dispatch.rs`), so 14c has no cross-file edit. 14c's `MainWindowInput::register` returns `Rc<MainWindowInput>` (14f's `MainWindowMenus::register` takes an `Rc`; stub edited).
- **D-W8B4X-9 Reaching features from outside the crate.** `MainWindow::with_feature::<T>(f)` is public (was `pub(crate)` on the inner type only). 14c exports `MainWindowInputFeature` (`input() -> &Rc<MainWindowInput>`); 14f (register line 4) and 17e (Fit closure for the overlay, Tools-menu host) get the handle with `with_feature`. `MainWindowOverlay` implements `MainWindowFeature` directly (`draw_overlay`, `on_changes` -> `on_view_model_changed`, `dispose`, `as_any`); no wrapper, no typed overlay field in `MainWindowInner` (resolves 13i Q2 vs 15j Q5 vs D-W8B4O-14a); 15j's `UiWake` finds it with `with_feature::<MainWindowOverlay>`.
- **D-W8B4X-10 `signed_low_word` / `signed_high_word` (e).** 14a's `shell_input::native::{signed_low_word, signed_high_word}(isize)` are the shared helpers (`Lparam` is `isize`); 16g imports them and the private twins are deleted from the stub. Hence 14a before 16g and the edge 16g -> 14a.
- **D-W8B4X-11 Images through the hub (a).** The 13g constructor subscribes `hub.subscribe_image_changed`; the handler body (13j `on_hub_image_changed`) only QUEUES the image and posts a Render-priority safety-net drain; `apply_changes` drains it after the view-model borrow ended (D-W8B4M-7). 16i's T2 ("the handler runs `composer.set_incoming`, `invalidate_layout`, `window.invalidate`") is rewritten accordingly; the hub itself is unchanged (it still raises synchronously, to a snapshot of handlers, with no hub borrow held). 13i calls `hub.note_frame_presented(contained_image)` after each present; 13j computes the flag.
- **D-W8B4X-12 Compare frame and `try_select_compare_at`.** 16b Q1 ("13j builds the frame from a view-model borrow inside `draw_presentation`") broke D-W8B4M-2. 13j now clones the two `Arc<DecodedImage>` of the compare cells and the selection flags under a short borrow, then builds `CompareFrame` by struct literal from the clones; `CompareFrame::from_view_model` serves 16b's own tests only. Separately: `try_select_compare_at` has NO production caller in the C# baseline (only `ShellViewingTests`; `ShellInput` returns `false` for any press while compare is visible), so the "Downstream: 14c" edge of 13j is removed; the method stays for the ported test (needs amendment A4).
- **D-W8B4X-13 `SinkCallbacks` / `PresentationSinkFactory` (g).** Decision: MOVE. `MainViewModelSinkCallbacks` (with the fifth closure `take_viewer_zoom_change`), `SetCurrentImageCallback` and `PresentationSinkFactory` go from `pr_shell::composition::contracts` to `pr_app::coordinators::sink_callbacks` (amendment A11); `pr-shell` re-exports them so 09a/09b text is unchanged. 16i then has `pub type SinkCallbacks = MainViewModelSinkCallbacks` and `impl PresentationSinkFactory for Win32PresentationSinkFactory`. Until A11 lands the 16i stub keeps its twin (the move needs a merged-code PR, so the stub is not edited) and 09a's "16i implements `PresentationSinkFactory`" stays false; the contract text of 16i Q1 says so.
- **D-W8B4X-14 Host twins (h).** `host::contracts::{SizeD, PointD}` and `host::frame_clock::base::{FrameTick, FrameHandler, FrameHandlerId}` are field-for-field copies of the `pr_app` types, and `pr-platform-win` already depends on `pr-app` (the `host/mod.rs` sentence "No `pr_app` type is used" is stale). Amendment A10 replaces the twins by `pub use` of the `pr_app` types; the conversions in 13j (`client_size_dip`), 14b/14e (`screen_to_client_dip`), 16b (`to_app_tick`), 16f/16g/16h then become identities. Until then there is ONE tick converter, `shell_viewing::crossfade::to_app_tick` (15h uses it for `Animator::on_frame`; 15h's contract did not say so) and one field copy per `SizeD`/`PointD` crossing.
- **D-W8B4X-15 Handler order.** 15f Q4 and 15j Q4 said "only 15j inserts first"; the C# has TWO `InsertMessageHandlerFirst` callers (`RegisterKeepWarm` in the constructor, `AttachOverlay` later), verified in the baseline. Chain from the head: overlay (15j, `attach` after the constructor), keep-warm (16d), scroll bars, input (keyboard, pointer, wheel), windowing `DisplayChangeHandler`, context menu. Keep-warm returns `None` for every message, so the two heads are not observably ordered; the real constraint is that 15j attaches after the constructor. 15f T2/Q4, 15j Q4/semantics 5 and 14c T4 are consistent now.
- **D-W8B4X-16 `Closed` ordering.** `MainWindowFeature` has no `on_closed` hook and needs none: each `register_*` line (scroll bars, input, windowing) subscribes the window's `Closed` itself, and `register_features` runs before the core's subscriptions, so the feature handlers run first (C# comment). 14c's drop-target revoke relies on this (HWND still valid in `WM_DESTROY`); `dispose` only repeats it.
- **D-W8B4X-17 Text renderer (13g Q10 vs 15j Q6).** Neither unit creates it: the caller of `MainWindowOverlay::attach` (17e) builds the `DWriteTextRenderer` and its id, as the C# startup did through `AttachOverlay`. 15j Q6 is rewritten; 16c's composer takes no text renderer.
- **D-W8B4X-18 `SettingsSource`.** Amendment A6 `MainViewModel::settings_source()` replaces "the composition root passes it in `FeatureOptions`" (13h Q5, 14c Q6).
- **D-W8B4X-19 Unwired `ShellInput` hooks.** Confirmed: `set_overlay_hit_test`, `set_activation_key_owned`, `set_escape_interceptor`, `subscribe_pointer_activity` have no production caller in the baseline; 14b's downstream note that 15j sets them is withdrawn; 15j only calls `apply_fit`.
- **D-W8B4X-20 Shared test fixtures.** `tests/common/main_window_fixture.rs` (13g..13k), `viewing_fakes.rs` (16b..16i), `overlay_rig.rs` (15h..15j), `shell_window_fixture.rs` (merged) are separate files, each created by the first unit that ports and extended sequentially. The `MainViewModel` for the window tests comes from the `#[path]` include of `pr-app/tests/support/main_env.rs` (its crates `pr-app`, `pr-core`, `pr-imaging`, `pr-l10n`, `pr-test-support` are all dependencies or dev-dependencies of `pr-platform-win` since 15j added `pr-l10n`; the `mod support { .. }` nesting of the including test file must mirror the C#-style layout of `viewmodels_main_commands.rs`); amendment A9 is the fallback if that does not compile.
- **D-W8B4X-21 `PrefetchCache` stays local** (16d Q1 default): `D2dGpuImageCache` implements it through `inner().contains`; no `PrefetchStatus::contains` is required (A12 is optional).
- **D-W8B4X-22 `KeepWarmWindow` and `OverlayWindowPort`.** Both are local traits implemented for `ShellWindow` by delegating to its inherent `insert_message_handler_first`; they do not conflict. Amendment A7 (`ShellWindowApi::{insert_message_handler_first, is_destroyed}`) would delete both; it is recommended, not blocking.
- **D-W8B4X-23 Service adapters.** `MainWindowInputServices` / `MainWindowMenuServices` are implemented by 14c / 14f (`Weak<MainWindowInner>` adapters over the `pub(crate)` accessors, 13g Q9/Q11); `OverlayViewModel` by 15j; `ForwardedOpenTarget` by 17e. Accessors needed: `window()` (`Rc<ShellWindow>` coerces to `Rc<dyn ShellWindowApi>`), `viewport()` (coerces to `Rc<dyn ViewportControllerApi>`), `dispatcher()` as `Arc::new(inner.dispatcher().clone())` (`Win32UiDispatcher: UiDispatcher + Clone`, verified), `with_view_model` over `update_view_model`. `impl ViewportControllerApi for ViewportController` (forwarders) is written by 14c.
- **D-W8B4X-24 Plan edges.** Applied in `docs/WAVE-8-PLAN.md` (only the 24 `Depends on` cells and one "B4 port order" paragraph; table below). Plan row 13i still lists `Program.cs 16` (-> W8-20a, D-W8B4M-13): the row text was left untouched on purpose.

### Required amendments to merged code (prerequisite PR; NOT implemented here)

All are additive except A10 and A11, which are mechanical moves. Contract = the unit that needs it.

| ID | File and exact proposed signature | Needed by |

### Not resolved (needs a product or lead decision)

- 16i: the dropped cross-thread guard of `Win32PresentationSink` (4 C# tests without twin): `Different (compile-time)` row without ADR (the contract's proposal) or an ADR (AGENTS.md says an intentional behaviour difference needs one).
- 14g: a paint panic in the owner-draw subclass procedure is swallowed and logged (C# parity, a confined R0003 exception) or captured and resumed after `TrackPopupMenuEx`.
- 14d: the drop effect ignores the source's allowed effects and the key state (C# parity) or honours them (a `Different` row); 14c/14d: the file opens inline in the OLE callback (C# parity) or is posted.
- 14f: no `SetForegroundWindow` / `WM_NULL` around `TrackPopupMenuEx` (C# parity) until a desktop run shows a menu that does not dismiss.
- 15j: where the dialogs behind `OverlayViewModel::{show_settings, show_skipped_files, show_recovery, show_diagnostics, show_benchmark, clear_cache, remove_duplicates}` come from. 15j specifies the trait and the impl over `MainWindow::update_view_model`, but the dialog services belong to batch B5/B6 (17e); the 17e contract must name the owner of these seven calls.
- One main window per thread (`with_current_window`) is assumed by 13g/13h/15j; a second window would need ids in the wake closures of the composition root.
- 14b G-INPUT replay and 14e's 12-configuration G-MENU need the golden syncs above (they need the C# oracle tool run, not a decision, but they block those assertions).

No native behaviour and no Rust test was run; the proposed amendments A1 to A13 were not written or compiled, so their exact bodies (and that A10's `PartialEq` of the `pr_app` types equals the twin tests' expectations, and that the A9 `#[path]` include compiles inside `pr-platform-win`) are unchecked. The stub edits were compiled (`cargo check`, clippy, rustfmt) but their bodies are `todo!()`. I read every README B4 section, the API / open-question / dependency / decision sections of all 24 contracts and the semantics of 13g, 13h, 14c, 16b, 16d; the remaining semantics sections were read only where a cross-reference grep pointed at them, so a contradiction hidden in a semantics paragraph I did not open may remain. Facts taken from the C# baseline by grep (two `InsertMessageHandlerFirst` callers, the `RegisterFeatures` order, `TrySelectCompareAt` callers) were checked in `C:/MyProjects/PhotoReview/src`, which is the working tree and may be newer than `d92d6aae`.

## Wave 8 batch B5 (dialogs and settings UI: `pr-platform-win::dialogs`, units W8-18a..18v and W8-19a..19m)

Contract-only batch: 35 contracts (`contracts/W8-18a.md` .. `W8-18v.md`, `W8-19a.md` .. `W8-19m.md`), stubs under `crates/pr-platform-win/src/dialogs/` (every body `todo!()`), 25 new oracle commands with inputs in `tests/golden-inputs/` and goldens in `tests/golden/` generated at C# `d92d6aae`. Nine contract groups wrote them (G1..G9); the per-group rosters and decision tables follow verbatim, then the lead reconciliation. The decision numbers keep the writers' blocks (1-11, 201-213, 301-320, 401-421, 501-520, 601-616, 701-715, 801-815, 901-916, lead 1001+); they are unique, and the contracts and stub docs cite them, so they were not renumbered.

Groups: G1 18a..18c (pickers, task dialog, services, clipboard); G2 18d..18g (template, chrome, edit centering, list dialog, control wrappers); G3 18h..18k (`NativeDialog` base); G4 18l..18o (painter, secondary window port); G5 18p..18s (zoom, diagnostics, action profiles, benchmark dialogs); G6 18t..18v (recovery dialog); G7 19a..19c (settings framework); G8 19d..19i (settings pages); G9 19j..19m (settings window).

### Wave 8 batch B5, group G1 (W8-18a, W8-18b, W8-18c): decisions for the lead to merge into `contracts/README.md`

Written by the contract writer; the lead may overrule. Where a question had a recommended default it was taken (standing instruction). Verified: the stubs pass `cargo check -p pr-platform-win --all-targets --locked`, `cargo clippy -p pr-platform-win --all-targets --locked -- -D warnings` and `rustfmt --edition 2024` (checked with the other writers' files as they were at the time). Nothing is ported: every body is `todo!()`.

#### Roster
| WU | Unit | Rust | Porter | Test kind / golden |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| W8-18a | `FolderPicker`, `NativeShellFolderDialog`, `NativeShellFileDialog` | `dialogs::{folder_dialog, file_dialog}` | Sonnet | N (6 native COM tests) + pure golden `folder-picker-policy` (`usable` and `pick` cases) |
| W8-18b | `TaskDialogs`, `Win32DialogService` (+ `ISecondaryWindowPort`, `SecondaryWindowKind`), `Win32ZoomPromptService`, `SecondaryWindowPortHost` | `dialogs::{task_dialog, dialog_service, zoom_prompt_service}` | Sonnet | F (11 C# tests as pure fakes) + oracle command `folder-picker-policy` + 1 native task dialog test |
| W8-18c | `Win32ClipboardService` | `dialogs::clipboard` | Sonnet | N (6 pure fake-API tests + 1 native) |

#### Decisions
- **D-W8B5-1 Owner window closures.** Every service that needs the owner window takes `Box<dyn Fn() -> Hwnd>` (`Hwnd = isize`, `0` = no owner window / destroyed) and calls it at USE time, never caches it. There is no shared alias: the composition root (17c/17e) builds one closure per service from the same `Weak` of the shell window (D-W8B3H-18 rule), exactly like the C# `Func<nint>` instances. `popup_host.rs` uses the same shape.
- **D-W8B5-2 Dialog failures.** The native pickers return `Result<Option<String>, Hresult>` (`Ok(None)` = cancelled by the user; `Err` = the C# `COMException`). `pr_core::abstractions::DialogService::pick_folder` has no error channel, so `Win32DialogService::pick_folder` logs `"Folder dialog failed"` with the `Hresult` (through an optional `Arc<dyn Log>` given with `with_log`) and answers `None`. C# lets the `COMException` escape to the caller's "guarded" wrapper; the visible difference (the caller sees "cancelled" instead of its error status) is recorded as a `Different` row in PARITY unless the lead prefers to add a `Result` to the pr-core trait (touches merged code and every caller; not taken).
- **D-W8B5-3 The secondary window port is declared in `dialog_service.rs`; the fallback is retired.** The C# declares `ISecondaryWindowPort` and `SecondaryWindowKind` in `Win32DialogService.cs`, so they are 18b's: `dialogs::dialog_service::{SecondaryWindowPort, SecondaryWindowKind}`. **W8-18o (`secondary_window_port.rs`, group G2?) must import them and implement the trait; it must not redeclare them.** `Lazy<ISecondaryWindowPort?>` becomes `Rc<LazySecondaryPort>` (factory run once on the first `get`, the answer, a port or `None`, is kept; shared by `Win32DialogService`, `Win32ZoomPromptService` and `SecondaryWindowPortHost`). The Rust factory is infallible (`FnOnce() -> Option<Rc<dyn SecondaryWindowPort>>`): the C# `catch` around `Lazy.Value` existed for the WPF bridge load, which is retired (WPF `SecondaryWindowFallback` 117 lines retired, plan "Retired C#"). With no port the service answers `false` (settings, batch review) or does nothing, as the C# did with a null fallback.
- **D-W8B5-4 External-editor and rename prompts are not ported.** `ShowExternalEditors`, `PromptAddExternalEditor`, `PromptDefaultExternalEditor`, `PromptRename` (C# members of `ISecondaryWindowPort` and `IDialogService`, default `null`) need `ExternalEditor`, `ExternalEditorsEdit` and `RenamePromptRequest`, none of which exists in Rust, and `pr_core::abstractions::DialogService` has no such members. No B5 row covers them (see "Unplanned C#" below). `SecondaryWindowKind::{ExternalEditors, RenamePrompt}` stay as enum variants (cheap, the router matches on them). Proposed follow-up: a small unit after B5 that adds the types to pr-core, the four default-`None` members to both traits, and the forwarding lines in `Win32DialogService`.
- **D-W8B5-5 Pick-folder title.** `Win32DialogService` gets the text of `dialog.pickFolder.title` (`pr_l10n::keys::DIALOG_PICK_FOLDER_TITLE`) from a `Box<dyn Fn() -> Arc<Localizer>>` evaluated per call, default `Localizer::current` (the C# `Tr.DialogPickFolderTitle` is the current language at call time); `with_localizer` lets tests choose the language without global state (D-W8-10c-3).
- **D-W8B5-6 Task dialog details.** (a) The main icon is the 16-bit `MAKEINTRESOURCE` value (`0xFFFD` information, `0xFFFF` warning for `Error`), not the sign-extended `nint` of the C# (W8-11c note 3 / D-W8B3-2). (b) The `TDN_CREATED` test hook runs inside `pending_panic::guarded` and a captured panic is resumed with `pending_panic::rethrow_pending()` after `TaskDialogIndirect` returns (D-W8B3H-4); the C# swallowed every exception from the hook. Not a product behaviour (the hook is test-only). (c) A process without comctl32 v6 is detected with `comctl::task_dialog::is_available()` before the config is built.
- **D-W8B5-7 Filter text is owned by Rust.** `Marshal.StringToCoTaskMemUni` + `FreeSpecs` become `FilterBuffers` (two `Vec<u16>` per filter and the `COMDLG_FILTERSPEC` array pointing into them). The buffers must outlive `IFileDialog::Show` (the dialog keeps the pointers); dropping them is the free.
- **D-W8B5-8 Clipboard seam.** `ClipboardApi::set_unicode_text` returns `Result<bool, Win32Error>` so the C# test that makes the fake throw (`ExternalException`) has a Rust twin; `Err` and `Ok(false)` are both `false` from `try_set_text`. `close` runs on every path after a successful `open`, through a guard, so a panic cannot leave the clipboard open.
- **D-W8B5-9 One new oracle command, `folder-picker-policy`.** `tools/oracle-cs/Commands/W8-18b.cs` (header comment has the dispatch line and the csproj line) compiles the baseline `FolderPicker.cs` into the oracle and records `UsableInitialFolder` (18 cases against a temp tree: trailing separator, `..`, other case, trailing spaces, `\\?\` prefix, forward slashes, drive root, `.`, a file, missing, invalid character, embedded NUL), what reaches the dialog from `Pick` (5 cases) and the English `Tr.DialogPickFolderTitle`. Inputs: `tests/golden-inputs/folder-picker-policy.json`. The rest of `TaskDialogs.cs`/`Win32DialogService.cs` cannot be compiled into the oracle (they use the `internal` `Shell.Interop` types), so those expectations are hand-written from `Win32DialogServiceTests`. Lead actions: (1) add to `Program.cs`: `// Commands\W8-18b` and `"folder-picker-policy" => Commands.W818bFolderPickerPolicyCases(inputs),`; (2) add to `OracleCs.csproj` (inside the `CSharpSrcDir` ItemGroup): `<Compile Include="$(CSharpSrcDir)\src\PhotoReview.Shell.Win32\Dialogs\FolderPicker.cs" Link="cs-baseline\FolderPicker.cs" />`; (3) run the oracle; (4) add the row to `tests/golden-inputs/README.md`: `| folder-picker-policy | W8-18a/18b FolderPicker | UsableInitialFolder, Pick forwarding, pick-folder title |`. The writer did NOT run the oracle and could not compile it against the baseline; the C# file is written against the public/internal surface read from the baseline sources.
- **D-W8B5-10 Native test conventions (this group).** Every test that creates a COM dialog, a task dialog or touches the real clipboard is `#[ignore = "native: needs desktop"]`, runs on its OWN thread (COM tests call `CoInitializeEx(COINIT_APARTMENTTHREADED)` first, drop all COM pointers, then `CoUninitialize`), joins with a bound (60 s), closes dialogs from a helper thread with `WM_CLOSE` until a 30 s deadline (the C# method), and never runs unbounded. The task dialog test holds `ActivationScope::from_manifest_text(comctl::SHELL_MANIFEST)` for the thread. The clipboard test restores the previous TEXT only and is run by hand.
- **D-W8B5-11 Composition notes (not implemented here).** (a) `ShellFolderPickerAdapter` (C# `ShellCompositionRoot`: `IFolderPicker` over `FolderPicker`) is W8-17c's: `IFolderPicker::pick_folder(&mut self, title, initial) -> Option<String>` = `FolderPicker::pick(title, initial)` with `Err` -> `None` + log (same rule as D-W8B5-2). (b) 17e creates ONE `Rc<LazySecondaryPort>` whose factory builds the 18o router, passes the same `Rc` to `Win32DialogService`, `Win32ZoomPromptService` and `SecondaryWindowPortHost`, and calls `SecondaryWindowPortHost::dispose()` on the UI thread before the owner window is destroyed. (c) `ZoomPromptOption(Rc<dyn ZoomPromptService>)` (14c) wraps `Win32ZoomPromptService`.

#### Missing plan edges (lead actions)
- **18b needs 18a** (code: `FolderPicker` is a constructor argument of `Win32DialogService`), **11c** (`comctl::task_dialog`, `ActivationScope`), **11a**, **13c** (`pending_panic`). The plan lists 11b, 04c only.
- **18c needs 04c** (`ClipboardService`) and **11a**; plan lists 11b only.
- **18a needs 11a** (`Hresult`, `Hwnd`); plan lists 11e only.
- **18o needs 18b** (the `SecondaryWindowPort` trait and `SecondaryWindowKind`), plan lists 18k, 10c. **18p** (`zoom_prompt`) is only reached through the router's `prompt_custom_zoom`.
- **18a (test) needs 18b's oracle output**: `tests/golden/folder-picker-policy.json` is generated from 18b's oracle command, so the lead runs it before 18a's test PR.
- **18a native tests need 11b** (`user32::{is_window_visible, post_message}`); **18b native test needs 11b** (`post_message`) and 11c (`ActivationScope`).
- Order inside the group: 18a, then 18b; 18c is independent.

#### Unplanned C# in the dialog tree (not owned by G1; listed so a row can be added)
`Dialogs/Native/ExternalEditorsDialog.cs`, `Dialogs/Native/RenamePromptDialog.cs`, `Dialogs/Native/TextPromptDialog.cs`, `Dialogs/Native/ExternalEditorProductName.cs` (FEAT-EXTERNAL-EDITORS / FEAT-RENAME: the dialogs behind the four prompt members of D-W8B5-4), and the types `ExternalEditorsEdit` (`Native`), `ExternalEditor` and `RenamePromptRequest` (Core / Abstractions) that the Rust side does not have yet. (Whether other files of `Dialogs/Native` and `Dialogs/SettingsUi` are unplanned is for the writers of 18d to 18v and 19.)

### Wave 8 batch B5, group G2 (dialog base: 18d template and chrome, 18e edit centering and list dialog, 18f/18g native controls): lead decisions

Written by the contract writer of group G2; the lead merges these lines into `contracts/README.md` ("Lead decisions (Wave 8 batch B5)") and may overrule. Where a question had a recommended default it was taken (standing instruction). Numbers `D-W8B5-201..213` are the block reserved for G2, so they cannot collide with the other B5 groups. Detail and reasons are in `contracts/W8-18d.md` .. `W8-18g.md`.
Verified while writing: the stubs under `crates/pr-platform-win/src/dialogs/native/` pass `cargo check -p pr-platform-win --all-targets --locked`, `cargo clippy -p pr-platform-win --all-targets --locked -- -D warnings` and `rustfmt --edition 2024 --check`; the x64 sizes and offsets of `LVCOLUMNW`, `LVITEMW`, `HDITEMW`, `NMCUSTOMDRAW`, `NMLVCUSTOMDRAW` (136, not the C# 104) and `DRAWITEMSTRUCT` equal the C# byte offsets (compile-time assertions, temporary probe file removed). The oracle file was NOT compiled or run.

| # | Decision | Where |
|---|---|---|
| D-W8B5-201 | **Native tests of the control wrappers do not need a `NativeDialog`.** The C# tests use `ControlGalleryDialog` (a `NativeDialog`); the Rust tests of 18d/18e/18f/18g create a hidden owner plus child windows by `template::window_class_name` and call the wrapper directly (fixtures `tests/common/dialogs_controls_fixture.rs`, created by 18f, and the merged `owner_draw_fixture.rs`). The tests that need the dialog (command routing, `WM_NOTIFY` routing, layout at two scales, leaks of the gallery, tooltips) are listed by C# name in `W8-18f.md` / `W8-18e.md` and ported by the W8-18k test porter in `tests/dialogs_native_controls_gallery.rs` and `tests/dialogs_native_list_dialog_e2e.rs` (the gallery dialog goes to `tests/common/dialogs_control_gallery.rs`). | 18d..18g |
| D-W8B5-202 | **Seams of `DarkDialogChrome`.** The C# class calls the static `NativeDialogChromePainter` (W8-18l..18n, which depends on 18d) and three OS helpers. Rust: trait `ChromePainter` (`attach_dialog`, `attach_control(hwnd, class)`, `attach_header_filler(header)`), implemented by the painter group for a unit struct (suggested name `NativeDialogChromePainter`, who does `ChromePaintKind`/`KindFor` inside `attach_control`), and trait `ChromeOs` (`apply_title_bar`, `combo_list_window`, `list_view_header`; real = `Win32ChromeOs`). `DarkDialogChrome::new(painter)` / `with_seams(painter, set_theme, os)`; no global `Instance` (W8-18h builds the shared `Rc<DarkDialogChrome>` with the production painter). The painter group must implement `chrome::ChromePainter`; if it names things differently the lead fixes one line in each contract. | 18d, 18l..18n |
| D-W8B5-203 | **`DIALOG_STYLE_CHILD` (`WS_CHILD \| WS_VISIBLE`) lives in `native::template`.** C# keeps `DialogStyles.Child` inside `ZoomPromptDialog.cs` (W8-18p), but `ListDialog` (18e) and every dialog use it; W8-18p `pub use`s it from `zoom_prompt.rs` instead of redefining. | 18d, 18p |
| D-W8B5-204 | **Template API shape.** `DialogControlClass` is `#[repr(u16)]` with the C# values; `DialogItem` owns its `String`; `build(...) -> Result<Vec<u8>, TemplateError>` (the one C# `ArgumentException`); `id as u16` truncation kept; no `AlignedTemplate` type: `dialog_native::user32::create_dialog_indirect_param` already copies into a 4-aligned buffer. A C# `null` title/text is unrepresentable (`""` has the same bytes). | 18d |
| D-W8B5-205 | **Control wrappers are unit structs with associated functions taking `Hwnd`; notification structures come as references.** `ComboBoxControl`, `ListBoxControl`, `ListViewControl`, `ProgressControl`, `TrackbarControl`, `ButtonStateControl`, `CommonControlsInit` mirror the C# static classes. `ListBoxControl::draw_item(&DRAWITEMSTRUCT, ..)`, `ListViewControl::custom_draw(&mut NMLVCUSTOMDRAW, ..)` and `draw_header_item(&NMCUSTOMDRAW, ..)` take safe references; the raw `lParam` cast (and its SAFETY) belongs to the dialog's `WM_DRAWITEM`/`WM_NOTIFY` handler (W8-18j/18k). Integer-only messages share `basic::send_int` (`pub(super)`, 18f); pointer messages use `dialog_native::user32::send_message` with a SAFETY comment each. | 18f, 18g, 18j, 18k |
| D-W8B5-206 | **Window property names carry the Rust prefix**: `PhotoReviewRs.EditCentering` (C# `PhotoReview.EditCentering`), and the same rule for the painter's `...ChromePaint`/`...ChromeDialog` props (18l..18n): a property is process-local, so this only keeps UI automation and diagnostics that look properties up apart from the C# app (precedent D-W8B3H-9). Subclass ids (`"EDCT"`, `"CHMP"`, `"CHMD"`) are unchanged. Not a behaviour difference; no `Different` row. | 18e, 18l..18n |
| D-W8B5-207 | **Subclass procedures use `host::pending_panic::guarded(0, ..)` instead of swallowing.** C# `EditTextCentering.Proc` wraps its body in `catch (Exception) {}` ("the box only keeps its top-aligned text"); Rust follows D-W8B3H-4 (capture the first payload per thread, return 0, the owner of the native call resumes it) because R0003 forbids a silent catch-all on the UI thread. Same rule for every `extern "system"` subclass or dialog procedure of B5. For a SUBCLASS procedure whose C# `catch` falls through to the final `DefSubclassProc` (as `EditTextCentering.Proc`), the Rust procedure also falls through to `def_subclass_proc` after a captured panic (the guard yields `Option`, not `0`), so the control's own `WM_NCDESTROY` handling never gets skipped. | 18e, all B5 native procedures |
| D-W8B5-208 | **`ListDialog` follows the BASELINE and plugs into the base class through a seam.** (a) The C# checkout is newer than `d92d6aae`: `ListDialog.cs` is 211 lines instead of 183 (`ForChoice`, `InitialSelectedIndex`, `SelectedIndex`, `OnOk`); those belong to the external-editors feature (`ExternalEditorsDialog`, `RenamePromptDialog`, `TextPromptDialog`, `ExternalEditorProductName`, none in the plan) and are NOT ported (P-17 style sync later). (b) The Rust `NativeDialog` base (W8-18h..18k) does not exist yet: `list_dialog.rs` holds the base-independent parts and the narrow trait `ListDialogBase` (the protected helpers the C# calls: `item`, `dx`, `dy`, `line_height`, `measure_text_width`, `measure_item_height`, `place`, `place_button_row`, `button_row_width`). AMENDED by D-W8B5-316 (G3): 18e itself writes `impl DialogContent for ListDialog` (the trait is in the 18h stub, so it compiles first; the stub of `list_dialog.rs` has the impl with `todo!()` bodies) and 18i writes `impl ListDialogBase for NativeDialog` (already in its stub); the plan edge is 18e -> 18h for the compile of `list_dialog.rs` only, the e2e tests are 18k's. `edit_centering.rs` has no such edge. | 18e, 18h..18k |
| D-W8B5-209 | **`NativeControls.cs` is cut at class boundaries, not at line 276.** 18f = lines 1-252 (`CommonControlsInit`, `ComboBoxControl`, `ListBoxControl`) in `controls/basic.rs`; 18g = lines 254-552 (`ListViewControl` in `controls/list_tree.rs`; `ProgressControl`, `TrackbarControl`, `ButtonStateControl` in `controls/custom.rs`). `controls/mod.rs` declares the three files and is final. `chrome::combo_list_window` is the single implementation of `ComboBoxControl.ListWindow` (18d, so the chrome does not wait for 18f). | 18f, 18g |
